Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the nano framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 202. The resulting operator achieves bound O(636/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_202(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 202. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^636(S, F) is trivial. QED.
